<agent>
verification_capability_repair_agent_compact_retry
</agent>

<task>
Act as the implementation owner for this bounded verification-capability repair. Analyze the real failed tool package and supplied current-run source bundle, then return complete create/replace file contents that implement or repair the semantic DUT harness and weight-binding contract. The executor will validate every path and expected hash, apply all edits atomically, materialize source hashes, and rerun the same capability tool. If the supplied source is insufficient, return status=blocked with exact missing facts instead of inventing a harness or claiming pass.
</task>

<rules>
1. This is an evidence-preserving compact implementation request selected before transport because the complete audit prompt exceeds the configured provider-safe size.
2. Use only the compact inputs and named constraints. The compact inputs preserve original evidence by path, sha256, schema/status fields, interface summaries, module headers, and source/compile indexes.
3. If the output schema requires file_edits, return complete content for create/replace edits. For operation=replace_text on an existing non-JSON source, use content='' and exact unique old_text/new_text anchors; no expected_sha256 is needed. The framework materializes anchored edits into the complete final file and rejects any missing, ambiguous, overlapping, or stale anchor. Never return ellipses, prose patches, or instructions for a human to finish.
4. For functional verification, random generation may create only the input stimulus. Expected output must be target-model inference using the same checkpoint and input; never accept random or RTL-derived golden data.
5. Do not infer DUT real-weight consumption from file existence. Require complete-scope tensor hashes, a bound loader/harness, and executed evidence.
6. If the current-run policy omits atol, rtol, or max_mismatch_fraction, accept the frozen framework loose defaults recorded in numeric_comparison_resolution. This initial resolution is authorized; never derive or loosen it from DUT output.
7. Preserve the numeric policy, comparison tolerance, checkpoint hash, stimulus hash, and expected-output hash throughout repair.
8. Board-level acceptance requires source-hash identity with the exact user sample-project wrapper; a simplified wrapper is not equivalent.
9. For board integration, exact simulation behavior is preserved by the hash-bound source files and compile authority; do not replace them with a behavioral or idealized model.
10. Compact JSON may use lossless columnar indexes and JSON-pointer $ref objects. Resolve column names, path_prefixes, views, and refs before reasoning; expand aliases and refs to real absolute paths in file_edits.
11. If compact inputs include checker_results, parameter_bindings, template_source_checks, attention_semantics, or cross_layer_trace, treat those fields as supplied evidence.
12. If compact inputs include candidate_verification_artifact_contract, candidate_verification_plan, retry_reconciliation_contract, or design_team, treat their visible status, summary, policy, node counts, error lists, and checker summaries as supplied Stage6 evidence; do not claim the evidence is absent merely because compact retry omitted full nested details.
13. If compact inputs include stage_gate_policy, report only current-stage blockers in risks and move later-stage obligations to proposed_actions.
14. Return conservative review findings; mark evidence as not_run only when the relevant field is absent from compact inputs.
15. All original task-specific rules below remain authoritative after compaction.
16. Return status=ready_to_apply only when file_edits contains a complete bounded implementation; otherwise return status=blocked and exact blocked_reasons.
17. Treat relevant_repair_experience only as an evidence-bound hypothesis prior. A validated_success episode never proves the current design, never expands edit authority, and must be rebound to the current sources/contracts and rerun through the same real-tool gate. Do not repeat a falsified intervention without current distinguishing evidence.
18. When relevant_project_knowledge is present, use it as a bounded project mental model, not as current proof. Reuse a claim only according to its epistemic_status, applicability, lifecycle_status, recorded source/contract identity, and evidence bindings. Current source, contract, and real-tool evidence always override it; a source_rebind_required claim must be re-established before it drives a repair, and no knowledge row expands edit authority or permits promotion.
19. Populate project_knowledge_updates only with genuinely new, refined, contradicted, or superseding understanding derived from the current hash-bound evidence. Capture durable operator semantics, ready/valid behavior, state-machine mechanism, timing relation, buffering/backpressure, memory mapping, or board lifecycle knowledge; cite exact evidence paths from the supplied package, state validity conditions and reuse guidance, and reference prior knowledge IDs when superseding or contradicting them. Return an empty list when this turn adds no durable understanding; do not restate unchanged context.
20. Every file edit must use a path allowed by repair_source_bundle.editable_contract. Use operation=create for a missing file with expected_sha256='', and operation=replace for an existing file with its exact supplied SHA-256.
21. Provide complete file contents, never snippets, prose patches, ellipses, or instructions for a human to finish.
22. For a .json target, return content='' and the complete object in json_content so the executor serializes it deterministically. For every non-JSON target, omit json_content and return complete string content.
23. Keep the repair bounded to the supplied verification-capability gap.
24. Use the single source-bound root candidate supplied by the current localized record.
25. A template correction is allowed only within the exact localized dependency pairs.
26. The current real-tool evidence has completed localization. Remove completed diagnostic regions delimited by SPATIALACC_TEMPLATE_TRACE_BEGIN/END from both paired template replacements while applying the functional correction, unless a named observation is still required to distinguish the proposed root cause. Do not remove permanent verification logic or functional RTL.
27. When prior_capability_repair_evidence exists, treat its earliest failing real-tool result as the current repair-loop observation. Repair that concrete failure in the existing agent-created files before proposing broader changes; do not repeat a create operation for an existing path.
28. When current_board_to_lower_layer_contradiction is status=proven, it is the authoritative current repair route. Repair only its target_debug_layer and earliest named boundary; do not redirect to historical board audits or ask to repeat an unrelated lower-layer replay.
29. Trusted templates are read-only because no template repair approval is present.
30. Do not return a template edit in this capability step. The compile-validated generated Scala templates are the read-only semantic baseline; template repair is reopened only after a real simulator failure localizes a DUT defect.
31. Existing emitted generated DUT SystemVerilog is read-only and may predate the compiled Scala baseline. Add Chisel adapter/elaboration sources only under generated/chisel/src/main/scala/spatialaccagent/semantic_harness, emit each stage into its isolated directory under generated/semantic_harness, and create or replace generated/memory/dut_weight_binding_manifest.json.
32. Instantiate the supplied compile-validated generated Scala template classes in standalone semantic-harness adapters so their actual weight/runtime ports remain observable; do not reimplement their datapath behavior in the wrapper and do not use pre-existing root emitted SystemVerilog as the semantic DUT baseline.
33. Each harness wrapper must instantiate the real generated DUT module(s), expose the standard ready/valid/data interface declared by the binding requirements, route the packed 32-bit real-weight stream into the DUT's actual weight storage/write ports, and make weight loading complete before input stimulus is accepted.
34. Implement the immutable stream_metadata_contract exactly: derive internal StreamBeat st/addr/last from accepted-beat counters and tensor shape; the external semantic testbench deliberately supplies only ready/valid/data.
35. Treat each stage requirement's weight_layout and contract_sha256 as authoritative. The framework materializes checkpoint values into 32-bit words already ordered by storage target, port-write address, and LSB-first word index; the wrapper must aggregate each declared contiguous target range into the declared DUT port width without reinterpreting tensor order or numeric encoding.
36. For fused attention, use the declared Q/K/V source_order, concat axis, tile order, and separate output-projection target exactly. For norm and bias targets, perform only the declared IEEE value-preserving storage cast. Never infer a different transpose, lane order, or packing.
37. Any repaired arithmetic must be synthesizable hardware. Do not use shortreal/real, DPI, host-language computation, unsynthesizable simulator-only arithmetic, or a wrapper-side behavioral model. Internal approximations are allowed only when they implement the frozen numeric contract closely enough to be judged by the unchanged real-model golden checker.
38. Use repair_source_bundle.trusted_numeric_support for IEEE arithmetic instead of inventing unverified floating-point operators. Its copied HardFloat and QuantCommon sources are read-only trusted dependencies in the generated Chisel project; the supplied build.sbt and build.properties are authoritative for compilation.
39. Use reference_operator_semantics, its hash-verified semantic adapter/model implementation, and captured same-inference mask/position/RoPE tensors as the semantic authority. Do not infer a different Qwen/OPT/model-family convention.
40. Do not implement a behavioral substitute, identity/default path, sampled-weight path, or output generator in the wrapper. Do not read expected/golden/reference files from harness source. Real simulator comparison must remain capable of exposing wrong DUT behavior.
41. The binding manifest must follow agent_manifest_schema and agent_manifest_example for verification_scope, preserve every previously certified lower-layer harness, identify top modules and exact interfaces, bind all required hashes, and disable default/identity weight fallback. Prefer one absolute source_directory per isolated harness; after runMain the executor discovers source files and recomputes all hashes.
42. Use the supplied exact semantic-testbench producer/consumer source as executable contract authority. One approved runMain may elaborate all stage tops sequentially, but each stage source_directory must be self-contained and free of duplicate module definitions within that directory.
43. This is the layer-2 connected single-transformer-layer repair. Preserve every certified operator-leaf harness and add or repair the required single_layer_harness; do not rerun or redesign passing leaf operators unless current connected real-tool evidence contradicts them.
44. The connected harness must instantiate the supplied compile-validated connected Transformer-block Scala DUT, not chain leaf verification harnesses and not reimplement any datapath. It may only adapt the canonical composite weight/runtime loaders, derive stream metadata, expose the real DUT output, and emit nonfunctional pipeline trace records.
45. Bind connected_weight_stream_contract_sha256, connected_runtime_stream_contract_sha256, pipeline_plan_sha256, every required tensor hash, and a complete loader_route_contract that covers every non-empty stage target/range exactly once. A global loader address must be translated to the selected target-local address and local last indication; do not forward the global final-word indication to every target.
46. Implement the v3 single_layer_pipeline_overlap_contract and its hash-bound trace ABI exactly. Emit bounded SPATIALACC_PIPELINE_TRACE first/last accepted-transfer records at every contract-listed ready/valid stream edge, using its exact boundary_id and per-boundary token/beat geometry. This is an elastic, rate-insensitive token pipeline: heterogeneous operator latency, initiation interval, backpressure bubbles, unequal start/end cycles, and failure to place every stage in the same cycle are expected and are not functional failures. Evidence must preserve token order, exclude every whole-sequence operator barrier, and show different tokens in flight concurrently across every contract edge marked overlap_requirement=required; any positive upstream token distance is valid. Edges marked diagnostic, per-stage turnover gaps, and maximum same-cycle occupancy are optimization evidence only and MUST NOT authorize a hardware repair. A trace limited to block input/first-stage/block output or layer-level overlap alone is still insufficient.
47. Only the connected single-layer scope may be claimed. Do not create, execute, or claim the board AXI/DDR layer-3 harness in this repair.
48. Do not edit input policy, checkpoint, model reference, expected outputs, semantic testbench generator, acceptance checkers, verification reports, certificates, SACG state, architecture, pipeline, memory layout, or runtime ABI.
49. Current-run generated artifacts may contain current model dimensions and module names; framework-core code must remain model-independent.
50. Applying the already frozen loose numeric defaults is authorized, but changing their values is forbidden.
51. Use requested_validation only for sbt Compile/compile and runMain spatialaccagent.semantic_harness.<MainObject> in the supplied generated/chisel cwd. Each request argv must contain exactly one sbt goal after the standard sbt flags: use the single argv item 'runMain spatialaccagent.semantic_harness.<MainObject>', not separate 'runMain' and class-name items. The executor validates and runs these commands without a shell, then always reruns the authoritative case-adapter capability tool.
52. Return one JSON object only.
</rules>

<stage>
repair_execution
</stage>

<agent>
verification_capability_repair_agent
</agent>

resizeSigned(x: SInt, width: Int): SInt = resizeSignedToUInt(x, width).asSInt\n\n  def resizeSignedToUInt(x: SInt, width: Int): UInt = {\n    val u = x.asUInt\n    if (width <= x.getWidth) {\n batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(hiddenSize % lanes == 0, \"hiddenSize must be divisible by lanes\")\n  require(elemBits == 16 || elemBits == 32, \"ElementwiseMul inputs must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(outputBits == 16 || outputBits == 32, \"ElementwiseMul output must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  val beats: Int = hiddenSize / lanes\n  val inputBeatBits: Int = lanes * elemBits\n  val outputBeatBits: Int = lanes * outputBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * beats max 2)\n}\n\nclass ElementwiseMul(p: ElementwiseMulParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val lhs = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val rhs = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.outputBeatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val inputSpec = StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.addrBits)\n  val outputSpec = StreamSpec(p.outputBeatBits, p.addrBits)\n  val lhsQ = Module(new Queue(\n    new StreamBeat(inputSpec),\n    4,\n    pipe = true,\n    flow = false\n  ))\n  val rhsQ = Module(new Queue(\n    new StreamBeat(inputSpec),\n    4,\n    pipe = true,\n    flow = false\n  ))\n  val outputQ = Module(new Queue(\n    new StreamBeat(outputSpec),\n    2,\n    pipe = true,\n    flow = false\n  ))\n\n  io.lhs <> lhsQ.io.enq\n  io.rhs <> rhsQ.io.enq\n  io.out <> outputQ.io.deq\n\n  val a = lhsQ.io.deq.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val b = rhsQ.io.deq.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val y = Wire(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.outputBits.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n    val product = IeeeMath.mulFp32(\n      IeeeMath.toFp32(a(i), p.elemBits),\n      IeeeMath.toFp32(b(i), p.elemBits)\n    )\n    y(i) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(product, p.outputBits)\n  }\n\n  val productBeat = Wire(new io.write.bits.data\n    stMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.st\n    lastMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.last\n  }\n\n  io.readAddr.ready := !readValid || io.read.ready\n  when(io.readAddr.fire) {\n    readData := dataMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readAddrReg := io.readAddr.bits\n    readSt := stMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readLast := lastMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readValid := true.B\n  }\n  when(io.read.fire) {\n    readValid := false.B\n  }\n\n  io.read.valid := readValid\n  io.read.bits.data := readData\n  io.read.bits.st := readSt\n  io.read.bits.addr := readAddrReg\n  io.read.bits.last := readLast\n}\n","content_sha256":"a179ec331fba4025c1b33f88d122fdd06f9f9a6bc50e2bf7d50ec570b12b3136","path":"accagent/framework/templates/operator_chisel/KVCache.scala","sha256":"a179ec331fba4025c1b33f88d122fdd06f9f9a6bc50e2bf7d50ec570b12b3136","truncated":false},{"bytes":6281,"content_chars":6281,"content_head":"package = 0\n) {\n  require(seqLen > 0, \"seqLen must be positive\")\n  require(slidingWindow >= 0, \"slidingWindow must be non-negative\")\n  require(globalEvery >= 0, \"globalEvery must be non-negative\")\n  val seqBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen + 1 max 2)\n  val rowBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen max 2)\n}\n\nclass AttentionMask(p: AttentionMaskParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(p.seqBits))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val rowIndex = Input(UInt(p.rowBits.W))\n    val mask = Output(UInt(p.seqLen.W))\n  })\n\n  val seqLimit = RegInit(p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W))\n  when(io.cfgValid) {\n    seqLimit := Mux(io.cfg.seqlen === 0.U || io.cfg.seqlen > p.seqLen.U, p.seqLen.U, io.cfg.seqlen)\n  }\n\n  val bits = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, Bool()))\n  val globalRow =\n    if (p.globalEvery > 0) io.rowIndex % p.globalEvery.U === 0.U else false.B\n  for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    val idx = i.U(p.rowBits.W)\n    val active = i.U < seqLimit\n    val causalKeep = if (p.causal) idx <= io.rowIndex else true.B\n    val slidingKeep =\n      if (p.slidingWindow > 0) (idx + p.slidingWindow.U) > io.rowIndex else true.B\n    bits(i) := active && causalKeep && (slidingKeep || globalRow)\n  }\n  io.mask := bits.asUInt\n}\n","content_sha256":"bfb70e48641d60c25100b8240d5fef461aa954847c5fe5777b7fbb5445fc0e0a","path":"accagent/framework/templates/operator_chisel/Mask.scala","sha256":"bfb70e48641d60c25100b8240d5fef461aa954847c5fe5777b7fbb5445fc0e0a","truncated":false},{"bytes":8824,"content_chars":8824,"content_head":"package FP32\")\n  val beats: Int = hiddenSize / lanes\n  val beatBits: Int = lanes * elemBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * beats max 2)\n  val streamCapacity: Int = beats + 1\n}\n\nclass false,\n    flow = false,\n    useSyncReadMem = true\n  ))\n  val outputQ = Module(new Queue(\n    new StreamBeat(streamSpec),\n    2,\n    pipe = true,\n    flow = false\n  ))\n\n  io.residual <> IeeeMath.toFp32(a(i), p.elemBits),\n      IeeeMath.toFp32(b(i), p.elemBits)\n    )\n    y(i) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(sum, p.elemBits)\n  }\n\n  val joinedBeat = Wire(new StreamBeat(streamSpec))\n constantsLoaded\n\n  when(io.runtime.fire) {\n    when(runtimeCount < p.cachePositionWordOffset.U) {\n      positionIds(runtimeCount) := io.runtime.bits.data\n    }.elsewhen(runtimeCount < f620f845978ac826142690d666660996f5c1100096ca51427a3cf","source_reference_sha256":"3d3aab3a74ccc0b4efa744570e3486aed39941b21e8ca1ae938ce32d50351d27","status":"pass"},"path":"accagent/runs/spatialacc_qwen_agent_fast_run/generated/memory/dut_weight_binding_manifest.json#single_layer_closure_binding_merge_authority","projection":true,"source_path":"accagent/runs/spatialacc_qwen_agent_fast_run/generated/memory/dut_weight_binding_manifest.json","source_sha256":"22bac0663a46ee2fa9e0e3fc5be05ef695ded76a388132d53803c8305282a22d","truncated":false},{"bytes":100196,"content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate semantic testbench vectors from a real-model reference run.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nREPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\nif str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))\n\nfrom accagent.framework.numeric_policy import numeric_comparison\nfrom accagent.framework.board_reference_output import select_board_reference_output\nfrom accagent.framework.board_validation_scope import resolve_board_validation_scope\nfrom accagent.framework.semantic_runtime import (\n    materialize_composite_runtime_stream,\n    materialize_semantic_runtime_constants,\n)\nfrom accagent.framework.transcendental_contract import materialize_transcendental_contract\nfrom accagent.framework.verification_evidence_contract import certificate_contract_errors\nfrom accagent.framework.weight_layout import (\n    build_stage_weight_layout,\n    materialize_composite_weight_stream,\n    materialize_stage_weight_stream,\n)\n\n\nSCHEMA_VERSION = \"spatialaccagent.semantic_testbench_manifest.v1\"\n\n\ndef read_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    with path.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"expected JSON object: {path}\")\n    return data\n\n\ndef write_json(path: Path, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with path.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(data, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True)\n        f.write(\"\\n\")\n\n\ndef sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:\n    digest = hashlib.sha256()\n    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b\"\"):\n            digest.update(chunk)\n    return digest.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef sha256_json(value: Any) -> str:\n    encoded = json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\")).encode(\"utf-8\")\n    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef safe_id(value: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"_\", value.lower()).strip(\"_\") or \"stage\"\n\n\ndef scalar(stage: dict[str, Any], name: str, default: int) -> int:\n    bound = stage.get(\"bound_params\", {}) if isinstance(stage.get(\"bound_params\"), dict) else {}\n    row = bound.get(name)\n    if isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"value\") is not None:\n        try:\n            return int(row[\"value\"])\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            pass\n    numeric = stage.get(\"numeric_contract\", {}) if isinstance(stage.get(\"numeric_contract\"), dict) else {}\n    if numeric.get(name) is not None:\n        try:\n            return int(numeric[name])\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            pass\n    return default\n\n\ndef numeric_comparison_policy(\n    policy: dict[str, Any],\n) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, Any]]:\n    return numeric_comparison(policy)\n\n\ndef tensor_words(tensor: Any, bits: int) -> list[int]:\n    import torch\n\n    value = tensor.detach().cpu().contiguous()\n    if bits == 16:\n        raw = value.to(torch.float16).view(torch.int16).reshape(-1).tolist()\n        return [int(item) & 0xFFFF for item in raw]\n    if bits == 32:\n        raw = value.to(torch.float32).view(torch.int32).reshape(-1).tolist()\n        return [int(item) & 0xFFFFFFFF for item in raw]\n    raise ValueError(f\"unsupported floating-point testbench width: {bits}\")\n\n\ndef write_packed_memh(path: Path, tensor: Any, bits: int, lanes: int) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    words = tensor_words(tensor, bits)\n    if len(words) % lanes:\n        raise ValueError(f\"tensor word count {len(words)} is not divisible by lanes={lanes}\")\n    mask = (1 << bits) - 1\n    width = bits * lanes\n    digits = (width + 3) // 4\n    lines = []\n    for offset in range(0, len(words), lanes):\n        packed = 0\n        for lane, word in enumerate(words[offset : offset + lanes]):\n            packed |= (word & mask) << (lane * bits)\n        lines.append(f\"{packed:0{digits}x}\")\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"ascii\")\n    return {\n        \"path\": str(path),\n        \"sha256\": sha256_file(path),\n        \"bits\": bits,\n        \"lanes\": lanes,\n        \"beats\": len(lines),\n        \"tensor_shape\": list(tensor.shape),\n        \"encoding\": f\"ieee_fp{bits}_lane0_lsb\",\n    }\n\n\ndef sv_identifier(value: Any, label: str) -> str:\n    text = str(value or \"\")\n    if not re.fullmatch(r\"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_$]*\", text):\n        raise ValueError(f\"invalid SystemVerilog identifier for {label}: {text!r}\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef verified_source_files(harness: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str]]:\n    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    for item in harness.get(\"source_files\", []):\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            errors.append(\"harness source-file entry is not an object\")\n            continue\n        path = Path(str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\"))\n        declared = str(item.get(\"sha256\") or \"\")\n        if not path.is_file():\n            errors.append(f\"harness source file is missing: {path}\")\n            continue\n        actual = sha256_file(path)\n        if actual != declared:\n            errors.append(f\"harness source hash mismatch: {path}\")\n            continue\n        rows.append({\"path\": str(path), \"sha256\": actual})\n    if not rows:\n        errors.append(\"harness has no hash-verified source files\")\n    return rows, errors\n\n\ndef write_weight_stream(path: Path, bindings: list[dict[str, Any]], bits: int = 16) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    import torch\n\n    if bits != 16:\n        raise ValueError(f\"semantic weight stream currently requires fp16 words, got {bits}\")\n    words: list[int] = []\n    tensor_layout = []\n    for tensor_id, binding in enumerate(bindings):\n        tensor_path = Path(str(binding.get(\"path\") or \"\"))\n        if not tensor_path.is_file() or sha256_file(tensor_path) != binding.get(\"file_sha256\"):\n            raise ValueError(f\"real-weight tensor file/hash mismatch: {tensor_path}\")\n        tensor = torch.load(tensor_path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=True)\n        values = tensor_words(tensor, bits)\n        if len(values) % 2:\n            values.append(0)\n        offset = len(words)\n        for index in range(0, len(values), 2):\n            words.append(values[index] | (values[index + 1] << 16))\n        tensor_layout.append(\n            {\n                \"tensor_id\": tensor_id,\n                \"tensor\": binding.get(\"tensor\"),\n                \"tensor_sha256\": binding.get(\"sha256\"),\n                \"word_offset\": offset,\n                \"word_count\": len(words) - offset,\n                \"packing\": \"two_ieee_fp16_values_per_u32_lane0_lsb\",\n            }\n        )\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(\"\".join(f\"{word:08x}\\n\" for word in words), encoding=\"ascii\")\n    return {\n        \"path\": str(path),\n        \"sha256\": sha256_file(path),\n        \"word_bits\": 32,\n        \"word_count\": len(words),\n        \"tensor_layout\": tensor_layout,\n    }\n\n\ndef semantic_harness_tb(\n    *,\n    tb_module: str,\n    harness: dict[str, Any],\n    input_vectors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    expected_output: dict[str, Any],\n    output_path: Path,\n    weight_stream: dict[str, Any] | None,\n    runtime_stream: dict[str, Any] | None,\n) -> str:\n    top_module = sv_identifier(harness.get(\"top_module\"), \"harness top module\")\n    interface = harness.get(\"interface\", {}) if isinstance(harness.get(\"interface\"), dict) else {}\n    clock_port = sv_identifier(interface.get(\"clock_port\"), \"clock port\")\n    reset_port = sv_identifier(interface.get(\"reset_port\"), \"reset port\")\n    input_ports = interface.get(\"inputs\", []) if isinstance(interface.get(\"inputs\"), list) else []\n    output_port = interface.get(\"output\", {}) if isinstance(interface.get(\"output\"), dict) else {}\n    if len(input_ports) != len(input_vectors):\n        raise ValueError(f\"harness input count {len(input_ports)} does not match vectors {len(input_vectors)}\")\n\n    declarations = [\"  reg clock = 0;\", \"  reg reset = 1;\"]\n    connections = [f\"    .{clock_port}(clock)\", f\"    .{reset_port}(reset)\"]\n    drive_lines: list[str] = []\n    handshake_lines: list[str] = []\n    done_terms: list[str] = []\n    for index, (port, vector) in enumerate(zip(input_ports, input_vectors)):\n        if not isinstance(port, dict):\n            raise ValueError(f\"input interface {index} is not an object\")\n        width = int(vector[\"bits\"]) * int(vector[\"lanes\"])\n        declared_width = int(port.get(\"data_width_bits\") or 0)\n        if declared_width != width:\n            raise ValueError(f\"input {index} width {declared_width} does not match encoded vector width {width}\")\n        valid_port = sv_identifier(port.get(\"valid_port\"), f\"input {index} valid\")\n        ready_port = sv_identifier(port.get(\"ready_port\"), f\"input {index} ready\")\n        data_port = sv_identifier(port.get(\"data_port\"), f\"input {index} data\")\n        declarations.extend(\n            [\n                f\"  reg [{width - 1}:0] input_mem_{index} [0:{int(vector['beats']) - 1}];\",\n                f\"  reg input_{index}_valid = 0;\",\n                f\"  wire input_{index}_ready;\",\n                f\"  reg [{width - 1}:0] input_{index}_data = 0;\",\n                f\"  integer input_{index}_accepted = 0;\",\n            ]\n        )\n        connections.extend(\n            [\n                f\"    .{valid_port}(input_{index}_valid)\",\n                f\"    .{ready_port}(input_{index}_ready)\",\n                f\"    .{data_port}(input_{index}_data)\",\n            ]\n        )\n        last_port = port.get(\"last_port\")\n        if last_port:\n            last_port = sv_identifier(last_port, f\"input {index} last\")\n            declarations.append(f\"  reg input_{index}_last = 0;\")\n            connections.append(f\"    .{last_port}(input_{index}_last)\")\n            drive_lines.append(f\"      input_{index}_last = input_{index}_accepted == {int(vector['beats']) - 1};\")\n        drive_lines.extend(\n            [\n                f\"      input_{index}_valid = input_{index}_accepted < {int(vector['beats'])};\",\n                f\"      if (input_{index}_accepted < {int(vector['beats'])}) input_{index}_data = input_mem_{index}[input_{index}_accepted];\",\n            ]\n        )\n        handshake_lines.append(\n            f\"      if (input_{index}_valid && input_{index}_ready) input_{index}_accepted = input_{index}_accepted + 1;\"\n        )\n        done_terms.append(f\"input_{index}_accepted == {int(vector['beats'])}\")\n\n    output_width = int(expected_output[\"bits\"]) * load_reference_tensors(reference: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[Any, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:\n    import torch\n\n    random_input = torch.load(reference[\"input\"][\"path\"], map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=True)\n    records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n    for row in reference.get(\"reference\", {}).get(\"operator_records\", []):\n        if not isinstance(row, dict) or not row.get(\"module\") or not row.get(\"tensor_path\"):\n            continue\n        data = torch.load(row[\"tensor_path\"], map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=True)\n        if isinstance(data, dict):\n            records[str(row[\"module\"])] = data\n    return random_input, records\n\n\ndef semantic_stage_map(reference: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:\n    value = stage map\")\n    return {str(key): row for key, row in value.items() if isinstance(row, dict)}\n\n\ndef stage_tensors(\n    op: str,\n    random_input: Any,\n    records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],\n    stage_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],\n) -> tuple[list[Any], Any]:\n    contract = stage_map.get(op)\n    if not isinstance(contract, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"model semantic adapter has no mapping for pipeline op {op}\")\n\n    def value(selector: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:\n        if selector.get(\"source\") == \"accelerator_input\":\n            return random_input\n        name = str(selector.get(\"record\") or \"\")\n        key = str(selector.get(\"value\") or \"\")\n        tensor = records.get(name, {}).get(key)\n        if tensor is None:\n            raise ValueError(f\"reference tensor is missing: {name}.{key}\")\n        return tensor\n\n    input_selectors = contract.get(\"inputs\", []) if isinstance(contract.get(\"inputs\"), list) else []\n    expected_selector = contract.get(\"expected\", {}) if isinstance(contract.get(\"expected\"), dict) else {}\n    if not input_selectors or not expected_selector:\n        raise ValueError(f\"semantic mapping for {op} has no inputs or expected selector\")\n    return [value(selector) for selector in input_selectors if isinstance(selector, dict)], value(expected_selector)\n\n\ndef required_weights(op: str, stage_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> list[str]:\n    contract = stage_map.get(op, {})\n    values = contract.get(\"required_weight_suffixes\", []) if isinstance(contract, dict) else []\n    if not isinstance(values, list):\n        raise ValueError(f\"semantic mapping required_weight_suffixes is malformed for {op}\")\n    return [str(value) for value in values]\n\n\ndef stream_metadata_contract() -> dict[str, Any]:\n    return {\n        \"schema_version\": \"spatialaccagent.semantic_stream_metadata.v1\",\n        \"scope\": \"all operator-leaf harness input streams\",\n        \"tensor_order\": \"row_major_with_feature_axis_last\",\n        \"preconditions\": [\n            \"each input tensor has at least one axis\",\n \"beats_per_logical_vector\": \"tensor_shape[-1] / lanes\",\n            \"logical_vector_count\": \"product(tensor_shape[:-1])\",\n            \"total_beats\": \"beats_per_logical_vector * logical_vector_count\",\n            \"logical_vector_index\": \"accepted_beat_index / beats_per_logical_vector\",\n            \"feature_beat_index\": \"accepted_beat_index % beats_per_logical_vector\",\n        },\n        \"internal_streambeat_fields\": {\n            \"data\": \"external data_port unchanged\",\n            \"st\": \"feature_beat_index == 0\",\n \"position_index_when_required\": (\n            \"logical_vector_index modulo configured sequence length; captured runtime position/rope tables remain \"\n            \"authoritative for semantic values\"\n        ),\n        \"counter_reset\": \"synchronous DUT reset; counters remain zero until all required loaders complete\",\n        \"transaction_order\": [\n            \"assert and release reset\",\n            \"load all real-weight words in ascending address order\",\n            \"load all runtime-constant words in ascending address order\",\n            \"pulse start for one harness_elaboration_contract(run_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    root = run_dir / \"generated\" / \"semantic_harness\"\n    return {\n        \"schema_version\": Any],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    stage_map = semantic_stage_map(reference)\n    stage_rows = []\n    weights = reference.get(\"weights\", {}).get(\"layer_0_tensor_bindings\", [])\n    by_suffix = {\n weight tensors\")\n    return {\n        \"schema_version\": \"spatialaccagent.dut_weight_binding_requirements.v1\",\n        \"status\": \"requirements\",\n        \"source_reference_manifest\": bind this accelerator catalog hash and contain every required tensor hash\",\n        },\n        \"policy\": {\n            \"weight_file_existence_alone_is_insufficient\": True,\n            \"every_required_tensor_must_reach_a_DUT_weight_storage_or_port\": True,\n            \"default_or_identity_initialization_cannot_pass\": True,\n            \"testbench_must_load_weights_before_stimulus\": True,\n            \"numeric_comparison_requires_explicit_atol_rtol_and_max_mismatch_fraction\": True,\n            \"transformer_blocks_are_the_only_dut_weight_scope\": True,\n            \"embedding_final_norm_lm_head_weights_are_not_required_by_dut\": True,\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef dut_binding_errors(\n    binding: dict[str, Any],\n    requirements: dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    require_single_layer: bool = True,\n) -> list[str]:\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    if binding.get(\"status\") != \"pass\":\n        errors.append(\"DUT weight-binding manifest status is not pass\")\n    if binding.get(\"accelerator_scope\") != \"transformer_blocks_only\":\n        errors.append(\"DUT weight binding accelerator_scope is not transformer_blocks_only\")\n    for key in (\n        \"source_checkpoint_sha256\",\n        \"source_reference_sha256\",\n        \"numeric_policy_sha256\",\n        \"model_semantic_adapter_sha256\",\n    ):\n        if binding.get(key) != requirements.get(key):\n            errors.append(f\"DUT weight binding {key} does not match verification preparation\")\n    if binding.get(\"scope_coverage_complete\") is not True:\n        errors.append(\"DUT weight binding does not claim complete verification-scope coverage\")\n    if binding.get(\"dut_consumes_bound_weights\") is not True:\n        errors.append(\"DUT weight binding does not prove that the bound weights are consumed\")\n    if binding.get(\"default_or_identity_weight_fallback_disabled\") is not True:\n        errors.append(\"DUT default/identity weight fallback is not disabled\")\n    required_hashes = {\n        str(row.get(\"sha256\"))\n        for row in requirements.get(\"single_layer_required_tensors\", [])\n        if isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"sha256\")\n    }\n    consumed_hashes = {str(value) for value in binding.get(\"consumed_tensor_hashes\", []) if str(value)}\n    missing_hashes = sorted(required_hashes - consumed_hashes)\n    if missing_hashes:\n        errors.append(f\"DUT binding is missing {len(missing_hashes)} required real-weight tensor hash(es)\")\n    stage_harnesses = binding.get(\"stage_harnesses\", {}) if isinstance(binding.get(\"stage_harnesses\"), dict) else {}\n    missing_stages = [\n        str(row.get(\"stage_id\"))\n        for row in requirements.get(\"stage_requirements\", [])\n        if isinstance(row, dict) and str(row.get(\"stage_id\")) not in stage_harnesses\n    ]\n    if missing_stages:\n        errors.append(f\"DUT binding has no semantic harness for stages {missing_stages}\")\n    if require_single_layer and not isinstance(binding.get(\"single_layer_harness\"), dict):\n        errors.append(\"DUT binding has no connected single-layer semantic harness\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef validate_harness_weight_hashes(\n    harness: dict[str, Any],\n    required_bindings: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    weight_layout: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    runtime_constant_stream: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> list[str]:\n    required = {str(row.get(\"sha256\")) for row in required_bindings if row.get(\"sha256\")}\n    consumed = {str(value) for value in harness.get(\"consumed_tensor_hashes\", []) if str(value)}\n    missing = sorted(required - consumed)\n    errors = [f\"semantic harness does not consume required tensor hashes {missing}\"] if missing else []\n    if required and harness.get(\"default_or_identity_weight_fallback_disabled\") is not True:\n        errors.append(\"semantic harness does not disable default/identity weight fallback\")\n    if required and isinstance(weight_layout, dict):\n        expected_layout_hash = str(weight_layout.get(\"contract_sha256\") or \"\")\n        if harness.get(\"weight_layout_contract_sha256\") != expected_layout_hash:\n            errors.append(\"semantic harness weight-layout contract hash does not match requirements\")\n    if isinstance(runtime_constant_stream, dict):\n        expected_runtime_hash = str(runtime_constant_stream.get(\"contract_sha256\") or \"\")\n        if harness.get(\"runtime_constant_contract_sha256\") != expected_runtime_hash:\n            errors.append(\"semantic harness runtime-constant contract hash does not match requirements\")\n        interface = harness.get(\"interface\", {}) if isinstance(harness.get(\"interface\"), dict) else {}\n        if not isinstance(interface.get(\"runtime_loader\"), dict):\n            errors.append(\"semantic harness has no runtime-constant loader interface\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef single_layer_pipeline_overlap_contract(\n    input_vector: dict[str, Any],\n    pipeline: dict[str, Any],\n    pipeline_plan_sha256: str,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    shape = [int(value) for value in input_vector.get(\"tensor_shape\", [])]\n    lanes = int(input_vector.get(\"lanes\") or 0)\n    if not shape or lanes <= 0 or shape[-1] % lanes:\n        raise ValueError(\"single-layer input shape/lanes cannot define token stream boundaries\")\n    token_count = 1\n    for size in shape[:-1]:\n        token_count *= size\n    beats_per_token = shape[-1] // lanes\n    if token_count < 2:\n        raise ValueError(\"pipeline-overlap verification requires at least two logical tokens\")\n    stages = [\n        str(row.get(\"stage_id\") or \"\")\n        for row in pipeline.get(\"stages\", [])\n        if isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"stage_id\")\n    ]\n    if not stages:\n        raise ValueError(\"pipeline-overlap verification requires planned spatial stages\")\n    stage_set = set(stages)\n    stream_edges = [\n        row\n        for row in pipeline.get(\"stream_edges\", [])\n        if isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"edge_id\")\n    ]\n    if not stream_edges:\n        raise ValueError(\"pipeline-overlap verification requires ready/valid stream edges\")\n\n    boundary_contracts = []\n    for edge in stream_edges:\n        stream = edge.get(\"stream_contract\", {})\n        stream = stream if isinstance(stream, dict) else {}\n        total_elements = int(stream.get(\"transfer_count_elements\") or 0)\n        element_bits = int(stream.get(\"element_bits\") or 0)\n        stream_beat_bits = int(stream.get(\"stream_beat_bits\") or 0)\n        if (\n            total_elements <= 0\n            or total_elements % token_count\n            or element_bits <= 0\n            or stream_beat_bits <= 0\n            or stream_beat_bits % element_bits\n        ):\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"pipeline edge {edge.get('edge_id')} cannot define per-token stream beats\"\n            )\n        elements_per_beat = stream_beat_bits // element_bits\n        elements_per_token = total_elements // token_count\n        if elements_per_token % elements_per_beat:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"pipeline edge {edge.get('edge_id')} has a partial per-token stream beat\"\n            )\n        boundary_contracts.append(\n            {\n                \"boundary_id\": str(edge[\"edge_id\"]),\n                \"src_stage\": str(edge.get(\"src_stage\") or \"\"),\n                \"dst_stage\": str(edge.get(\"dst_stage\") or \"\"),\n                \"kind\": str(edge.get(\"kind\") or \"\"),\n                \"flow_control\": str(edge.get(\"flow_control\") or \"\"),\n                \"beats_per_token\": elements_per_token // elements_per_beat,\n            }\n        )\n\n    stage_activity_contracts = []\n    for stage_id in stages:\n        inputs = [\n            row[\"boundary_id\"]\n            for row in boundary_contracts\n            if row[\"dst_stage\"] == stage_id\n        ]\n        outputs = [\n            row[\"boundary_id\"]\n            for row in boundary_contracts\n            if row[\"src_stage\"] == stage_id\n        ]\n        if not inputs or not outputs:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"pipeline stage {stage_id} lacks an observable input or output boundary\"\n            )\n        stage_activity_contracts.append(\n            {\n                \"stage_id\": stage_id,\n                \"input_boundaries\": inputs,\n                \"output_boundaries\": outputs,\n            }\n        )\n    internal_boundaries = [\n        row\n        for row in boundary_contracts\n        if row[\"src_stage\"] in stage_set and row[\"dst_stage\"] in stage_set\n    ]\n\n    def has_alternate_path(candidate: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n        adjacency: dict[str, list[str]] = {stage_id: [] for stage_id in stages}\n        for row in internal_boundaries:\n            if row[\"boundary_id\"] == candidate[\"boundary_id\"]:\n                continue\n            adjacency[row[\"src_stage\"]].append(row[\"dst_stage\"])\n        pending = [\n            stage_id\n            for stage_id in adjacency.get(candidate[\"src_stage\"], [])\n            if stage_id != candidate[\"dst_stage\"]\n        ]\n        visited: set[str] = set()\n        while pending:\n            stage_id = pending.pop()\n            if stage_id == candidate[\"dst_stage\"]:\n                return True\n            if stage_id in visited:\n                continue\n            visited.add(stage_id)\n            pending.extend(adjacency.get(stage_id, []))\n        return False\n\n    dependency_edges = []\n    for row in internal_boundaries:\n        transitive_bypass = has_alternate_path(row)\n        dependency_edges.append(\n            {\n                \"boundary_id\": row[\"boundary_id\"],\n                \"src_stage\": row[\"src_stage\"],\n                \"dst_stage\": row[\"dst_stage\"],\n                \"kind\": row[\"kind\"],\n                \"relationship\": (\n                    \"transitive_bypass\" if transitive_bypass else \"direct_dataflow\"\n                ),\n                \"overlap_requirement\": (\n                    \"diagnostic\" if transitive_bypass else \"required\"\n                ),\n            }\n        )\n    block_inputs = [\n        row[\"boundary_id\"]\n        for row in boundary_contracts\n        if row[\"src_stage\"] == \"block_input\"\n    ]\n    block_outputs = [\n        row[\"boundary_id\"]\n        for row in boundary_contracts\n        if row[\"dst_stage\"] == \"block_output\"\n    ]\n    if not block_inputs or len(block_outputs) != 1:\n        raise ValueError(\"pipeline plan requires block-input edges and one block-output edge\")\n    trace_abi = (\n        \"SPATIALACC_PIPELINE_TRACE boundary=<id> cycle=<decimal> token=<decimal> beat=<decimal> \"\n        \"st=<0_or_1> last=<0_or_1> valid=<0_or_1> ready=<0_or_1>\"\n    )\n    legacy_v2_contract = {\n        \"schema_version\": \"spatialaccagent.single_layer_pipeline_overlap_contract.v2\",\n        \"pipeline_plan_sha256\": pipeline_plan_sha256,\n        \"stage_order\": stages,\n        \"planned_stage_count\": len(stages),\n        \"token_count\": token_count,\n        \"beats_per_token\": beats_per_token,\n        \"trace_abi\": trace_abi,\n        \"required_boundaries\": [row[\"boundary_id\"] for row in boundary_contracts],\n        \"boundary_contracts\": boundary_contracts,\n        \"stage_activity_contracts\": stage_activity_contracts,\n        \"dependency_edges\": [\n            {\n                \"boundary_id\": row[\"boundary_id\"],\n                \"src_stage\": row[\"src_stage\"],\n                \"dst_stage\": row[\"dst_stage\"],\n            }\n            for row in dependency_edges\n        ],\n        \"block_input_boundaries\": block_inputs,\n        \"block_output_boundary\": block_outputs[0],\n        \"acceptance\": {\n            \"all_required_records_are_accepted_transfers\": True,\n            \"every_token_has_first_and_last_record_at_each_required_boundary\": True,\n            \"maximum_next_token_stage_entry_gap_cycles\": 1,\n            \"every_stage_emits_token_0_before_accepting_the_final_token\": True,\n            \"every_adjacent_stage_pair_overlaps_on_different_tokens\": True,\n            \"all_planned_spatial_stages_are_concurrently_active_after_fill\": True,\n            \"minimum_concurrent_stage_count\": len(stages),\n            \"whole_sequence_barrier_forbidden\": True,\n        },\n        \"policy\": {\n            \"trace_is_observation_only\": True,\n            \"trace_must_not_change_ready_valid_or_data\": True,\n            \"transaction_counts_alone_cannot_prove_pipeline_overlap\": True,\n            \"stage_activity_is_derived_from_actual_module_port_handshakes\": True,\n            \"layer_level_overlap_cannot_substitute_for_intra_layer_operator_overlap\": True,\n        },\n    }\n    legacy_v2_contract[\"contract_sha256\"] = sha256_json(legacy_v2_contract)\n\n    trace_contract = {\n        \"schema_version\": \"spatialaccagent.single_layer_pipeline_trace_contract.v1\",\n        \"pipeline_plan_sha256\": pipeline_plan_sha256,\n        \"trace_abi\": trace_abi,\n        \"required_boundaries\": [row[\"boundary_id\"] for row in boundary_contracts],\n        \"boundary_contracts\": boundary_contracts,\n        \"stage_activity_contracts\": stage_activity_contracts,\n        \"token_count\": token_count,\n    }\n    trace_contract_sha256 = sha256_json(trace_contract)\n    required_dependency_count = sum(\n        row[\"overlap_requirement\"] == \"required\" for row in dependency_edges\n    )\n    contract = {\n        \"schema_version\": \"spatialaccagent.single_layer_pipeline_overlap_contract.v3\",\n        \"pipeline_semantics\": \"elastic_rate_insensitive_token_pipeline\",\n        \"pipeline_plan_sha256\": pipeline_plan_sha256,\n        \"stage_order\": stages,\n        \"planned_stage_count\": len(stages),\n        \"token_count\": token_count,\n        \"beats_per_token\": beats_per_token,\n        \"trace_abi\": trace_abi,\n        \"trace_contract\": trace_contract,\n        \"trace_contract_sha256\": trace_contract_sha256,\n        \"compatible_trace_contract_sha256s\": [\n            trace_contract_sha256,\n            legacy_v2_contract[\"contract_sha256\"],\n        ],\n        \"required_boundaries\": [row[\"boundary_id\"] for row in boundary_contracts],\n        \"boundary_contracts\": boundary_contracts,\n        \"stage_activity_contracts\": require(totalBeats % beatsPerVector == 0)\n\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val enable = Input(Bool())\n    val externalValid = Input(Bool())\n    val externalReady = Output(Bool())\n    val externalData = Input(UInt(dataBits.W))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(dataBits, addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  private val acceptedBeat = RegInit(0.U(addrBits.W))\n  private val io.out.bits.data := io.externalData\n  io.out.bits.st := featureBeat === 0.U\n  io.out.bits.addr := acceptedBeat\n  io.out.bits.last := featureBeat === (beatsPerVector - 1).U\n\n  when(io.out.fire) writeCount: Int\n) extends Module {\n  require(wordsPerWrite > 0)\n  require(writeCount > 0)\n\n  private val wordIndexBits = log2Ceil(wordsPerWrite max 2)\n  private val writeAddrBits = Output(Bool())\n  })\n\n  private val words = Reg(Vec(wordsPerWrite, UInt(32.W)))\n  private val wordIndex = RegInit(0.U(wordIndexBits.W))\n  private val writeIndex = RegInit(0.U(writeAddrBits.W))\n  private val pendingWrite = RegInit(false.B)\n  private val allWritesLoaded = RegInit(false.B)\n\n  io.in.ready := !pendingWrite && !allWritesLoaded\n\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    words(wordIndex) := io.in.bits\n    when(wordIndex === (wordsPerWrite - 1).U) {\n      wordIndex := 0.U\n      pendingWrite := true.B\n    }.otherwise {\n      wordIndex := wordIndex + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := pendingWrite\n  io.out.bits.addr := writeIndex\n  io.out.bits.data := words.asUInt\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    pendingWrite := false.B\n    when(writeIndex === (writeCount - 1).U) {\n      writeIndex := 0.U\n      allWritesLoaded := true.B\n    }.otherwise {\n      writeIndex := writeIndex + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  io.loaded := allWritesLoaded\n}\n\nprivate class ConnectedWeightLoader externalLast = Input(Bool())\n\n    val rms1Weight = Decoupled(UInt(256.W))\n    val qkvWeight = Decoupled(new WeightWrite(1024, log2Ceil(16128)))\n    val qkvBias = Decoupled(new WeightWrite(256, down.io.in.bits := io.externalData\n\n  private val selectedReady = MuxCase(\n    false.B,\n    Seq(\n      selectRms1 -> rms1.io.in.ready,\n      selectQkvWeight -> qkvWeight.io.in.ready,\n      selectQkvBias -> qkvBias.io.in.ready,\n      selectAttentionOut -> attentionOut.io.in.ready,\n      selectRms2 -> rms2.io.in.ready,\n      selectGate -> gate.io.in.ready,\n      selectUp ->
